The cograph then is #92;#123;#92;#123;7,1',9'#92;#125;,#92;#123;6,7',10',11'#92;#125;,#92;#123;5,2',8'#92;#125;,#92;#123;4,6'#92;#125;,#92;#123;3,3',4'#92;#125;,#92;#123;2,5'#92;#125;#92;#125;.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The dual of the graph of a function. It is an ordinate-indexed partition of the disjoint union of the set of abscissas and ordinates of the function."],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":2682195,"upstream_offset":7946953454,"upstream_record_sha256":"ab5b9439fb1df9ea24de8e469183724ff0c34d928fbe6e7e19538e78e673fc32","word":"cograph"}
